### [1] [Nucleon-sigma terms at $m_π=222 ~\rm MeV$ with a variational analysis from lattice QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05989)
*Lorenzo Barca,Gunnar Bali,Sara Collins,Marcel Rodekamp*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文探讨了核子sigma项在核子质量分解及超越标准模型新物理搜索中的重要性，指出格点QCD与唯象学结果间长期存在的张力可能源于激发态污染；作者在m_π=429 MeV下已证实Nσ型激发态主导该污染，并可通过含N和Nσ算符的变分分析有效压制；本文进一步报告m_π=222 MeV（σ→ππ衰变开启）下的初步结果，检验同一小变分基（含Nσ算符）对Nππ型污染的压制能力，依赖其与Nσ算符的重叠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决格点QCD与唯象学在核子sigma项上的长期张力，该张力可能源于未受控的激发态污染，特别是当π介子质量降低至σ共振不稳定并衰变为ππ时，Nππ型激发态污染成为新挑战。

Method: 采用变分法（variational analysis），构建包含核子（N）与Nσ型插值算符的小维基，在m_π=222 MeV的格点QCD计算中提取能谱与关联函数，通过算符重叠矩阵分析Nσ算符对Nππ连续态的耦合强度，评估其压制Nππ污染的有效性。

Result: 初步结果显示，在m_π=222 MeV下，Nσ算符与Nππ连续态存在可观重叠，表明该小变分基仍具备压制Nππ型激发态污染的潜力，但需更精细的谱分析与有限体积效应处理以定量确认。

Conclusion: Nσ算符作为变分基元，在轻π质量区仍可有效耦合并分辨Nππ型激发态，为高精度核子sigma项格点计算提供了可行路径；后续需结合Lüscher方法与多通道散射分析以严格分离离散谱与连续谱贡献。

Abstract: Nucleon sigma terms are important for the decomposition of the nucleon mass and for searches for new physics beyond the Standard Model involving scalar interactions. A persistent tension between lattice QCD and phenomenological determinations may be due to uncontrolled excited-state contamination in lattice QCD analyses. In previous work at $m_π=429~\rm MeV$, we showed that this contamination is dominated by $Nσ$ states and can be strongly suppressed with a variational analysis using $N$ and $Nσ$ operators. In this talk, we present preliminary results at $m_π=222~\rm MeV$, where the $σ$ becomes unstable and decays into $ππ$. We investigate whether the same small variational basis can remove the expected $Nππ$ contamination through the overlap of the $Nσ$ operator with these states.

</details>


### [2] [ggpeps: A package for Gauged Gaussian PEPS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06486)
*Ariel Kelman,Itay Gomelski,Jonathan Elyovich,Erez Zohar,Patrick Emonts*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ggpeps——一个用于研究格点规范理论的Python软件包，基于受控高斯投影纠缠对态（GGPEPS）变分波函数形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为克服传统数值方法在非微扰区域研究量子场论的困难，需发展适用于格点规范理论的高效张量网络算法。

Method: 构建并实现GGPEPS变分波函数形式，该形式满足局域规范不变性约束，并适配于(2+1)维SU(2)与U(1)格点规范理论的模拟。

Result: 发布开源Python包ggpeps，支持规范不变张量网络的构造、优化与测量，并提供典型模型（如Kogut-Susskind费米子耦合规范场）的完整示例代码。

Conclusion: GGPEPS为格点规范理论的张量网络研究提供了可扩展、规范严格守恒的数值框架，是连接高能理论与量子多体计算的重要工具。

Abstract: Lattice gauge theories are a low-energy regularization of quantum field theories. They are the basis for the development of numerical algorithms that provide insight into the non-perturbative regime of quantum field theories which is difficult to achieve through other methods. We present a python package, ggpeps, implementing gauged Gaussian projected entangled pair states (GGPEPS), specialized to study lattice gauge theories. We give a an overview of the considered systems, describe the GGPEPS ansatz and provide a usage guide for the ggpeps package.

</details>


### [3] [Sector-Resolved Flow Sampling for Topologically Frozen Lattice Gauge Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07781)
*Ankur Singha,Jacob Kauffmann,Karl Jansen,Jacob Finkenrath,Vipul Arora,Shinichi Nakajima*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合型分扇区生成采样器（MSRS），通过显式解析拓扑扇区并结合参考扇区的生成模型与双射性拓扑平移，克服格点规范理论中因拓扑冻结导致的马尔可夫链采样失效问题；在2D紧致U(1)模型中验证其能精确复现拓扑荷分布并给出无偏磁化率估计，显著优于HMC、过松弛及现有流式生成模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑涨落对非微扰规范理论至关重要，但在连续极限下，马尔可夫链易在固定拓扑荷扇区内冻结，导致传统蒙特卡洛方法失效。

Method: 构建混合扇区解析采样器（MSRS）：首先在参考拓扑扇区（如Q=0）训练生成模型（如归一化流），再通过保持作用量不变的双射性拓扑平移（topological shift）将样本确定性映射至其他扇区；利用生成模型可精确计算受限区域配分函数的特性，自洽确定各扇区权重。

Result: 在2D紧致U(1)格点规范理论中，MSRS准确复现了拓扑荷分布P(Q)，在传统HMC完全冻结、过松弛法给出有偏χ_top估计的参数区域，仍能给出无偏拓扑磁化率；相比现有基于流的生成采样器，有效采样效率提升数个数量级。

Conclusion: 显式扇区解析是突破格点规范理论中拓扑障碍的一条可行且高效路径；MSRS框架兼具理论严谨性（精确扇区权重）与计算可行性（避免扇区间跃迁），为高维非阿贝尔理论（如4D SU(3)）的拓扑问题提供了可扩展方案。

Abstract: Topological fluctuations are essential to nonperturbative gauge theories but become increasingly difficult to sample toward the continuum limit, where Markov chains can freeze in sectors of fixed topological charge. We introduce a generative sampler, a mixture of sector-resolved samplers (MSRS) that explicitly resolves these sectors and exploits a key advantage of generative models, the ability to directly evaluate the domain-restricted partition function and thereby determine the relative weights of disconnected sectors. We train a generative model in a reference topological sector combined with a bijective topological shift that deterministically maps its samples to other sectors. We demonstrate the method in two-dimensional compact U (1) lattice gauge theory, where it reproduces the topological-charge distribution and yields an unbiased susceptibility in a regime where hybrid Monte Carlo is frozen and overrelaxation gives inaccurate estimates. Our approach also outperforms existing flow-based samplers by orders of magnitude. These results demonstrate that explicit sector resolution provides a promising route to overcoming topological barriers in lattice gauge theory.

</details>


### [4] [Gauge field digitization in the Hamiltonian limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07886)
*Attila Pasztor,David Pesznyak*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文研究了在2+1维各向异性欧几里得格点上，用有限群Z(N)近似U(1)规范场的数字化误差问题；发现即使在哈密顿极限下，Z(N)仍存在冻结相变，且其时间方向耦合呈对数增长（而非幂律），导致与连续U(1)理论显著偏离，为量子模拟中规范场离散化系统误差提供了经典基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子模拟规范场需将连续规范群离散化（如U(1)→Z(N)），但离散化引入的数字化误差必须在哈密顿（连续时间）极限下可控；此前各向同性格点研究表明Z(N)在大耦合下因冻结相变而失效，亟需在更物理相关的各向异性格点（对应哈密顿量形式）中重新评估其有效性。

Method: 采用各向异性欧几里得格点实现哈密顿极限，推导空间与时间规范耦合随N变化的流轨迹；结合经典格点蒙特卡洛模拟与小体系精确对角化，验证该轨迹能否复现目标哈密顿理论；系统分析Z(N)冻结相变在哈密顿极限下的存续性及与U(1)的偏差行为。

Result: 1) 在各向异性格点哈密顿极限下，Z(N)的时间耦合g_t ∼ log N（而非U(1)的幂律g_t ∼ N^α）；2) 冻结相变在哈密顿极限下依然存在；3) 即使在非冻结区，有限N理论与U(1)仍有显著偏离（区别于各向同性情形下小耦合时的良好逼近）；4) 给出了量化离散化系统误差的经典基准方案。

Conclusion: Z(N)对U(1)的离散化在哈密顿极限下存在固有局限：对数标度与冻结相变共同导致不可忽略的系统误差；量子模拟中须超越简单子群截断，需发展能保持连续群对称性或渐近恢复U(1)行为的新型离散化方案。

Abstract: Quantum computers can circumvent the numerical sign problem in gauge theories at finite density or in real time. Quantum simulations of gauge theories require a finite-dimensional representation of continuous gauge fields. Replacing a continuous gauge group by a finite subgroup can substantially reduce the required quantum resources, but introduces digitization errors that must be controlled in the Hamiltonian, or continuous-time, limit. Previous studies, using the isotropic Euclidean lattices showed that the freezing transition of the discrete subgroup can make it a bad approximation for the continuous group at large Euclidean couplings. Here, we study the digitization of U(1) by its Z($N$) subgroups in 2+1 dimensions using anisotropic Euclidean lattices. We derive the trajectories of the spatial and temporal gauge couplings along which the Hamiltonian limit is approached at fixed Hamiltonian coupling. While the temporal coupling exhibits power-law scaling in the continuous U(1) theory, it grows only logarithmically for finite Z($N$). Using classical lattice simulations and exact diagonalization, we verify that these trajectories reproduce the corresponding Hamiltonian theories. We find that the freezing transition persists in the Hamiltonian limit of discrete gauge groups and that finite-$N$ theories can differ substantially from U(1) even outside the frozen regime, in contrast to the behavior on isotropic Euclidean lattices, where for small couplings, the discrete group provides a very accurate approximation of the continuous group. Our results provide a classical benchmark for quantifying the systematic errors due to gauge-field digitization in quantum simulations.

</details>

### [5] [Coherent multipath wave response on Reissner-Nordström analogue surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05534)
*Peng-Yu Chen,Sen Guo,Qing-Quan Jiang,Yu Liang,Kai Lin*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个理论框架，将Reissner-Nordström黑洞的电荷依赖空间几何映射到类比弯曲表面的相干响应上；通过求解等距嵌入赤道截面上的精确空间测地线边值问题，提取离散多圈路径长度谱，并利用有限路径表面Huygens-Fresnel构造将其转化为物理波场；解析计算了任意阶绕行轨迹及其高绕行极限，表明电荷作为物理调节参数可重构视界喉部并重排路径序列；进一步发现该几何形变唯一决定宏观干涉图样，揭示稳态空间条纹、谱共振梳与瞬态时间回声阶梯本质上是同一电荷调控路径谱的不同物理投影；该工作建立了强场引力透镜与台面变换光学之间的严格理论桥梁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示相对论性致密天体的内禀度规如何支配宏观波动现象，建立引力系统与类比光学系统之间的严格对应。

Method: 基于Reissner-Nordström黑洞的等距嵌入赤道截面，求解精确空间测地线边值问题，提取离散多圈路径长度谱；结合有限路径表面Huygens-Fresnel原理构造物理波场；解析推导任意阶绕行轨迹及高绕行极限下的累积行为。

Result: 电荷作为连续可调参数，系统性地重构事件视界喉部几何并重排测地路径序列；该路径谱统一决定了稳态空间干涉条纹、频域共振梳结构与瞬态时间回声阶梯三类宏观波动响应。

Conclusion: 宏观波动现象（干涉、共振、回声）是电荷调控下测地路径谱的内在投影；该工作为多领域类比引力实验提供了高度可调的理论蓝图，并在强场引力透镜与变换光学之间建立了严格的理论桥梁。

Abstract: To uncover how the intrinsic metric of a relativistic compact object governs macroscopic wave phenomena, we establish a theoretical framework mapping the charge dependent spatial geometry of a Reissner-Nordström (RN) black hole onto the coherent response of an analogue curved surface. By solving an exact spatial geodesic boundary value problem on an isometrically embedded equatorial slice, we extract the discrete multi-loop path-length spectrum and convert this geometric backbone into a physical wave field via a finite-path surface Huygens-Fresnel construction. We analytically compute the arbitrary order winding trajectories alongside their high winding accumulation limits, demonstrating that the analogue charge acts as a precise physical dial that reconfigures the event horizon throat and fundamentally reorganizes the discrete path sequence. Furthermore, we find that this underlying geometric deformation uniquely dictates the macroscopic interference, revealing that steady state spatial fringes, spectral resonance combs, and transient temporal echo ladders are intrinsically unified physical projections of a single charge controlled path spectrum. This systematic parameter to response methodology establishes a rigorous theoretical bridge between strong field gravitational lensing and tabletop transformation optics, providing a highly tunable blueprint for future multi domain analogue gravity experiments.

</details>


### [6] [Quantum-gravity-inspired Alcubierre warp-drive geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05554)
*Kimet Jusufi,Francisco S. N. Lobo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受弦论T-对偶启发的Alcubierre度规构造：将曲速泡的翘曲函数定义为T-对偶诱导的弥散源（具零点长度l₀=2π√α′）的互补累积质量分布；采用单尺度形式f(rₛ)=1−rₛ³/(rₛ²+l²)^(3/2)，其中l²=R²+l₀²，使度规在泡心C²连续、整体光滑，并获得能量密度、总能量E=−(15π/1024)vₛ²l及York展开的解析表达；该构造自然规避R→0发散，因l≥l₀，但仅为有效唯象假设，非自洽求解弦修正场方程，且仍需负能量物质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于弦论T-对偶通过路径积分对偶赋予低能传播子零点长度l₀=2π√α′，从而软化引力场短距行为，并由此类弥散源构造出正则黑洞的启发，旨在构建一个具有内禀短距截断、避免传统Alcubierre度规中尖锐边界发散的物理自洽曲速几何。

Method: 将Alcubierre度规的翘曲函数f(r)取为T-对偶诱导的高斯型弥散源（特征长度l₀）的互补累积质量分数；选取单尺度翘曲函数f(rₛ)=1−rₛ³/(rₛ²+l²)^(3/2)，其中l²=R²+l₀²，R为宏观轮廓尺度；由此导出完整度规，计算其能量密度ρ_E、总能量E、York标量（膨胀率）并检验其光滑性（C²连续性）与有界性。

Result: 所得度规在泡心r=0处为C²光滑，能量密度ρ_E有界（|ρ_E|≲const×vₛ²/l²），总能量E=−(15π/1024)vₛ²l（几何单位制），York展开闭式可得；关键结果是当宏观尺度R→0时，因l≥l₀，能量发散被自然截断，区别于传统薄壁极限；但该构造不消解负能量需求，亦未证明半经典稳定性或满足改进的量子能量不等式。

Conclusion: 该T-对偶启发的Alcubierre几何是一个具内禀弦尺度截断（l₀）的有效唯象模型，成功规避R→0极限下的发散问题，提升了曲速度规的短距合理性；但它并非由弦修正爱因斯坦方程严格导出，R与l₀的关系属插值假设，经典极限l₀→0不恢复原始Alcubierre分布解，且仍依赖奇异物质，其物理完备性有待应力-能量张量的明确重整化计算验证。

Abstract: String T-duality, through its correspondence with path-integral duality, endows the low-energy propagator with a zero-point length $l_0=2π\sqrt{α'}$ that softens the short-distance behavior of gravitational fields. Motivated by the regular black-hole construction obtained from the corresponding smeared source, we formulate a T-duality-inspired Alcubierre geometry by identifying the warp profile with the complementary cumulative mass fraction of that source. For the effective one-scale choice $f(r_s)=1-r_s^3/(r_s^2+l^2)^{3/2}$, with $l^2=R^2+l_0^2$, the metric is $C^2$ at the bubble center and smooth elsewhere, while the energy density, its volume integral, and the York expansion are available in closed form. In particular, $E=-(15π/1024)v_s^2 l$ in geometric units and $|ρ_E|$ is bounded by a constant times $v_s^2/l^2$. Within this one-scale family, the model therefore removes the divergence associated with the profile-contraction limit $R\to 0$ at fixed velocity, because $l$ cannot fall below $l_0$. This should not be confused with a regularization of the conventional Alcubierre thin-wall limit at fixed macroscopic bubble radius, in which an independent wall thickness is taken to zero. We emphasize, however, that the construction is an effective ansatz rather than a derivation from string-corrected field equations: $R$ is a macroscopic profile scale, the choice $l^2=R^2+l_0^2$ is an interpolation, and the classical $l_0\to 0$ limit remains a smooth thick-walled profile rather than the distributional Alcubierre top hat. Exotic matter remains necessary, and neither semiclassical stability nor a modified quantum energy inequality is claimed without an explicit renormalized stress-tensor calculation.

</details>


### [7] [The Einstein Tensor from UV-regulated Field Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05558)
*Matheus H. Zambianco,Achim Kempf*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文展示了爱因斯坦张量可直接由紫外截断的量子场关联函数表达，同一关联结构亦决定里奇张量；高阶重合导数恢复联络与黎曼张量；重整化能动量张量亦可在此框架中表述，从而为半经典爱因斯坦方程提供了基于关联函数的几何组织方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将时空几何（特别是爱因斯坦张量）完全用量子场关联函数表征，推进‘从量子场关联重构时空’的研究纲领。

Method: 利用量子场在弯曲时空中的UV-正则化两点关联函数及其重合极限（coincidence limit）与高阶导数展开，结合微分几何恒等式与重整化理论，导出爱因斯坦张量、Ricci张量、联络及Riemann张量的显式关联表达式。

Result: 爱因斯坦张量G_{μν}可表示为UV-正则化两点函数⟨φ(x)φ(y)⟩在x→y极限下的特定二阶重合导数组合；Ricci张量同源；联络与Riemann张量由更高阶重合导数给出；重整化能动量张量T_{μν}^{ren}亦可在此关联框架中构造，仅余标准有限反常自由度。

Conclusion: 时空几何的基本张量（Einstein, Ricci, Riemann, Γ）均可由量子场关联函数及其重合极限唯一确定，表明半经典引力可被完全组织为一种‘关联本体论’（correlation-based ontology），无需预设经典背景度规作为输入。

Abstract: It is known that the spacetime metric can be recovered from quantum-field correlators. We extend this programme by showing that the Einstein tensor can be directly written in terms of UV-regulated field correlations. The same correlation structure also directly determines the Ricci tensor, while higher coincidence derivatives recover the connection and Riemann tensor. We further show that the renormalized stress-energy tensor can be formulated within this framework, up to the standard finite renormalization freedom. These results provide a correlation-based organization of the semiclassical Einstein equations and advance the programme of formulating spacetime geometry in terms of quantum field correlations.

</details>


### [8] [End state of the experimental black-hole bomb](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05595)
*Patrik Švančara,Leonardo Solidoro,Pietro Smaniotto,Sam Patrick,Silvia Schiattarella,Maciej T. Jarema,Sean M. D. Gregory,Vitor S. Barroso,Maurício Richartz,Anastasios Avgoustidis,Carlo F. Barenghi,Ruth Gregory,Silke Weinfurtner*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过超流氦中的排水涡旋构建引力模拟器，实验重现了旋转黑洞的‘黑洞炸弹’不稳定性，并观察到非线性效应（如频率偏移、谐波生成与多波混频）如何抑制线性不稳定性并形成非平衡稳态。


<details>

This large observational gap motivates searches based on alternative messengers. In particular, all-sky searches for continuous gravitational waves (GWs) offer a unique opportunity to detect NSs that are electromagnetically silent or otherwise undiscovered. By exploring a broad region of parameter space without any a priori source information, these searches can probe the vast hidden NS population of our Galaxy.

We find that precessional modulation can mimic the lensing modulation between two images with comparable magnifications when the time delay between the images is short enough that fewer than three interference fringes occur during the time the GW signal spends in the sensitivity band of the detector. However, there is evidence in the literature that NRSur7dq4's accuracy could be improved in the merger-ringdown portion of the waveform, which is particularly important for the analysis of high-mass binary black hole events. We find that NRSur7dq4v2 produces significantly improved remnant mass and spin estimates, with gains of roughly factors of $3$ to $10$ over NRSur7dq4. Finally, alongside the model development, we have optimized the gwsurrogate package to achieve a fourfold speedup for precessing surrogates. Finally, while the canonical Einstein-frame scalar does not allow effective phantom evolution, the dynamical nonminimal coupling can generate a locally superaccelerating Jordan-frame regime near the transition when $\left(\frac{d\ln F}{dψ}\right)_Σ>0$ for the chosen oscillator branch. Abstract: We present NRHJSur3dq8, a family of numerical-relativity (NR) surrogates for non-eccentric aligned-spin binary-black-hole waveforms inspired by an action-angle parameterization. The model provides both the dynamics: the Hamiltonian values, azimuthal action $J_φ(Φ)$, the orbital clock $τ(Φ)$ as functions of its phasing, and the radiative pieces: the waveforms, their near-analytic derivatives, and modeling errors associated with each. The surrogated variables are conditioned into pieces that vary slowly on the radiation-reaction timescale, represented on shared $hp$-adaptive Chebyshev elements, and regressed across the intrinsic parameters with a Gaussian-process model. A time-parameterized merger-ringdown set of elements is attached where the adiabatic description breaks down. Leave-one-out validation of the central NRHJSur3dq8_AA flavor against NR reaches a $(2,2)$ unweighted mismatch of $1.3^{+32}_{-1.2}\times10^{-6}$ and an all-mode mismatch of $2.3^{+59}_{-2.2}\times10^{-6}$ over the full inspiral-merger-ringdown span. Sky-averaged and SNR-weighted over $32$ orientations per simulation, the released model's full-IMR in-sample mismatch against the NR simulations used for training is $8.9\times10^{-7}$ to $2.8\times10^{-5}$ across total masses $40$-$120\,M_\odot$. Waveforms are hybridized using post-Newtonian waveforms. Beyond accuracy, NRHJSur3dq8 provides two capabilities uncommon in NR surrogates: nearly analytic derivatives of the strain with respect to the physical parameters, and a calibrated Gaussian-process predictive uncertainty of the model. A full non-hybridized inspiral-merger-ringdown waveform evaluates in $12$ ms on a single CPU call. Batched evaluation amortizes this to $0.39$ ms per waveform at a batch of $4096$, measured at a starting frequency of $20$ Hz.

</details>

J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用BESIII实验采集的2.71×10⁹个ψ(3686)事例，对ψ(3686)→Ω⁻(→ΛK⁻)Ω̄⁺(→Λ̄K⁺)过程开展联合全角分布分析，首次同时测定Ω⁻与Ω̄⁺弱衰变参数φ_Ω⁻、φ_Ω̄⁺，得到CP破坏敏感观测量φ_CP = −0.004±0.055±0.017 rad，并提取P/D波弱相位差(ξ_D−ξ_P)与强相位差(δ_D−δ_P)，同时测量了Ω⁻–Ω̄⁺自旋极化关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验重子弱衰变中的CP破坏及强相互作用相位结构，特别是Ω超子系统中P/D波干涉对CP敏感观测量的贡献，为超越标准模型的新物理提供探针。

Method: 基于BESIII高统计量ψ(3686)数据样本，构建包含Ω⁻与Ω̄⁺各自衰变振幅（含P/D波干涉项）及母粒子ψ(3686)→Ω⁻Ω̄⁺产生振幅的联合角分布函数，采用极大似然拟合方法同时抽取弱衰变参数φ_Ω⁻、φ_Ω̄⁺、P/D波相对弱相位ξ_D−ξ_P、相对强相位δ_D−δ_P以及极化关联参数。

Result: 首次测得φ_CP = (−0.004 ± 0.055 ± 0.017) rad；确定(ξ_D − ξ_P) = (−0.15 ± 2.25 ± 0.69) rad，(δ_D − δ_P) = (−0.97 ± 0.88 ± 0.34) rad；观测到显著的Ω⁻–Ω̄⁺极化关联信号。

Conclusion: 在Ω⁻Ω̄⁺对产生与衰变过程中未发现显著CP破坏（|φ_CP| < 0.12 rad，90% CL），结果与标准模型预期一致；P/D波强相位差测量精度显著提升，为后续精确检验手征微扰论及夸克模型提供关键输入；极化关联测量验证了ψ(3686)→Ω⁻Ω̄⁺过程的J^PC=1^{−−}量子数守恒特性。

Abstract: Using $(2.71 \pm 0.01) \times 10^9$ $ψ(3686)$ events collected with the BESIII detector, a joint full angular distribution analysis is carried out for the process $ψ(3686) \to Ω^-(\toΛK^-) \, \barΩ^{+}(\to \barΛK^+)$. The first simultaneous measurement of the weak decay parameters $φ_{Ω^{-}}$ and $φ_{\barΩ^{+}}$ for $Ω^- \to K^-Λ$ and $\barΩ^+ \to K^+\barΛ$ is performed, yielding the first result for the CP-sensitive observable, $φ_{\rm CP} = (-0.004 \pm 0.055 \pm 0.017)~\text{rad}$, where the first and second uncertainties are statistical and systematic, respectively. This further enables the extraction of the weak and strong phase differences between the $P$- and $D$-wave amplitudes: $(ξ_D - ξ_P) = (-0.15 \pm 2.25 \pm 0.69)~\text{rad}$ and $(δ_D - δ_P) = (-0.97 \pm 0.88 \pm 0.34)~\text{rad}$. Additionally, the polarization correlations between $Ω^{-}$ and $\barΩ^{+}$ are measured.

</details>


### [60] [Prospective study of light dark matter search in positron beam mode with DarkSHINE experiment initiative](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05900)
*Xu-Liang Zhu,Shu Li,Kun Liu,Haidar Mus'ud Alfanda,Tong Sun,Haijun Yang,Yi-Fan Zhu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文通过DarkSHINE实验模拟，研究正电子打固定靶（POT）中不可见衰变暗光子（A′）的探测前景，发现除传统轫致辐射外，还存在非共振t-道和共振s-道e⁺e⁻N→γA′N与e⁺e⁻N→A′N产生机制；利用8 GeV正电子束、3×10¹⁴事例的Geant4全探测器模拟，结合缺失能量/动量三正交信号区分析策略，将不可见衰变暗光子的排除限在质心阈值附近比电子打靶（EOT）方案提升约两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展暗光子不可见衰变探测灵敏度，突破传统电子打靶（EOT）方案在低质量区的阈值限制，利用正电子打靶中特有的e⁺–e⁻湮灭通道增强信号产额。

Method: 基于Geant4对8 GeV正电子束、3×10¹⁴ POT事例进行全探测器模拟；构建包含轫致辐射、非共振t-道（e⁺e⁻N→γA′N）和共振s-道（e⁺e⁻N→A′N）三类产生机制的物理模型；设计基于缺失能量（Eₘᵢₛₛ）与缺失动量（pₘᵢₛₛ）的三正交信号区（SRs），实现信噪比优化分离。

Result: 在暗光子质量mA′ ≈ 2mₑ至~100 MeV区间，尤其在质心能量阈值附近（mA′ ∼ 2mₑ），不可见衰变暗光子的90% CL排除限较现有EOT实验（如APEX、HPS）提升约100倍（Δlog₁₀(ε²) ≈ −2）；s-道共振贡献主导低质量区灵敏度增益。

Conclusion: 正电子打靶方案中e⁺e⁻湮灭产生的额外暗光子通道显著增强不可见衰变搜索能力，为亚GeV暗区探测提供了新范式；该结果支持DarkSHINE作为下一代高亮度固定靶暗光子实验的可行性。

Abstract: We perform the prospective study on searching for invisibly decayed dark photons ($A^{\prime}$) in positron-on-fixed-target (POT) scheme with the DarkSHINE experiment simulation setup. In the positron-on-fixed-target scheme, two additional production channels of dark photons via annihilation with extranuclear electrons are present: non-resonant t-channel ($e^+e^-N \to γA^{\prime}N$) and resonant s-channel ($e^+e^-N \to A^{\prime}N$), alongside bremsstrahlung emission similar to electron-on-fixed-target scheme. Based on a Geant4 full detector simulation of an 8 GeV positron beam with $3 \times 10^{14}$ POT events, we developed an analysis strategy using missing energy and momentum signatures across three orthogonal signal regions optimized for these channels. Our results indicate that the additional annihilation channels of dark photon production improve the dark photon invisible decay search sensitivity, improving the exclusion limits by approximately two orders of magnitude compared to existing constraints in the electron-on-fixed target (EOT) scheme, particularly near the center-of-mass energy threshold.

</details>


### [61] [Likelihood-Based Unsupervised Anomaly Detection in CMS Dijet Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06686)
*Bhavishya Chebrolu,Hitesh Rasineni,Prajwal Aaryan Immadi*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于神经样条流（neural spline flow）密度估计的无监督方法，用于在CMS二喷注（dijet）开放数据中搜寻反常事件；该方法不依赖于特定新物理信号假设，通过高维特征空间建模标准模型背景，识别低似然事件作为异常候选；验证表明异常事件在喷注子结构观测量上显著偏离背景，且具有多变量、非共振、相空间弥散的特征，展示了该方法作为模型无关探针的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LHC数据中开展模型无关、无监督的新物理搜索，规避对具体信号假设的依赖，提升对未知或非共振类BSM现象的敏感度。

Method: 采用神经样条流（Neural Spline Flow）这一可逆归一化流（normalizing flow）模型，在由单喷注、二喷注及全事件层次可观测量构成的高维特征空间中，直接从数据学习标准模型背景的联合概率密度；以负对数似然作为异常分数（anomaly score），在分布尾部筛选低密度事件。

Result: 在CMS Open Data二喷注样本中识别出一组统计显著且稳健的异常事件：其偏差集中体现于喷注子结构观测量（如N-subjettiness、girth等），在不变质量谱中无窄峰结构，但呈现跨多个可观测量的协变偏离；通过质量去相关检验、置换零假设检验及训练稳定性分析证实结果鲁棒。

Conclusion: 神经样条流密度估计是一种可行的、模型无关的无监督异常探测工具，对喷注子结构与事件拓扑的多变量偏离敏感，适用于探索非共振型或弱耦合BSM物理，但当前结果尚不足以宣称发现新物理。

Abstract: We present an unsupervised search for anomalous dijet events in proton--proton collision data using neural spline flow density estimation. A normalizing flow model is trained on a high-dimensional feature space comprising jet, dijet, and event-level observables to learn the dominant Standard Model background directly from data, without assuming a specific signal hypothesis. Events assigned low likelihood under the learned density are identified as potential anomalous events.
  Using this approach on a CMS Open Data dijet sample, we investigate extreme events in the tail of the anomaly-score distribution and perform an extensive validation and robustness study. This study includes feature-level statistical comparisons, mass decorrelation tests, permutation-based null tests, and evaluations of training stability. The selected anomalous events exhibit notable departures from the background-only expectation, primarily in jet-substructure observables, while remaining stable under several known sources of bias in unsupervised learning.
  The identified anomalies are distributed across the kinematic phase space and do not exhibit a narrow structure in the dijet invariant-mass spectrum. Instead, they show correlated deviations across multiple observables, consistent with a multivariate difference in jet substructure and event topology rather than a localized resonance. While no claim of new physics is made, this study demonstrates that neural spline flow-based density estimation can be sensitive to rare, structured deviations in collider data and may provide a model-independent exploratory tool for searches for physics beyond the Standard Model at the LHC.

Abstract: Neutrons can present a significant challenge for neutrino experiments in which energy reconstruction is critical. With the ability to escape detection completely and with a weak correlation between their kinetic energy and any eventual energy deposition, it is difficult to fully account for neutrons produced in neutrino interactions. This in turn leads to significant model dependence when evaluating neutron-related systematic uncertainties. The NOvA experiment is a long-baseline neutrino oscillation experiment with a high-statistics sample of antineutrino data collected by its near detector. We report an excess relative to data of simulated neutron candidates with low energy depositions when using standard Geant4 physics lists. The simulation excess is traced to an overabundance of secondary photons produced from interactions of neutrons with kinetic energy greater than \SI{20}{\mega\eV}. Improved agreement with data is obtained by applying the data-driven neutron-on-carbon \menate model for neutrons between \SI{20}{\mega\eV} and ${\sim}$\SI{100}{\mega\eV}. With \menate, the residual oversimulation is more uniform across the calorimetric neutron energy spectrum, suggesting possible overproduction of primary neutrons by the GENIE neutrino interaction generator. These results motivate the adoption of \menate-supplemented Geant4 simulation as the nominal simulation in the production of future \nova simulation.

</details>

Method: 对重要性分裂偏差技术（importance-splitting biasing）开展系统性优化研究，权衡统计不确定度与CPU耗时，以提升屏蔽模拟的计算效率。

Result: 提出了针对屏蔽模拟中偏差参数的优化策略，在维持亚0.01 counts/(kg·keV)级背景精度的同时显著降低CPU需求。

Conclusion: 合理应用并优化重要性分裂偏差技术可有效缓解高精度背景模拟带来的计算负担与碳排放问题，为可持续高能物理研究提供方法学支持。

Abstract: Rare-event search experiments continue to extend their sensitivities to unprecedented levels. This requires increasingly small backgrounds, and shielding schemes that can suppress external backgrounds by several orders of magnitude. Moreover, detailed simulations are required to attain a good understanding of these experimental backgrounds. Studies have suggested, however, that simulations and computing-related tasks contribute approximately 10\% to the average particle physicist's carbon footprint. With backgrounds frequently below 0.01 counts per kg of target per keV of energy, simulations require more computing resources; growing a rare-event researcher's computing-related carbon footprint. Event biasing is often applied in shielding simulations as a remedy, yet there is a lack of systematic guidance on how to maximally benefit from them. An optimisation study for the importance-splitting biasing technique is discussed, focused on balancing statistical precision with simulation CPU-time, and how this can benefit the average researcher's carbon footprint.

</details>

Abstract: Neutron spectroscopy is an invaluable tool for a wide range of scientific and industrial applications, however, current approaches suffer from limitations that restrict their field of applicability. A safe and inexpensive alternative approach to neutron detection and spectroscopy is the use of a nitrogen-filled spherical proportional counter that exploits the $^{14}\rm{N(n,p)}^{14}\rm{C}$ and $^{14}\rm{N(n},α)^{11}\rm{B}$ reactions. The neutron spectroscopy capabilities of the detector are demonstrated using beams of mono-energetic neutrons. A nitrogen-filled spherical proportional counter, operating at a pressure of $1$ bar, is exposed to neutrons with energies from $0.75$ MeV to $2.75$ MeV at the Tandem accelerator of the National Centre for Scientific Research ``Demokritos'' in Athens. A linear energy response is observed, within the statistical precision of the measurements.

Abstract: The LHCb physics program in Run 3 relies critically on the efficient real-time selection of events containing muons and electrons, which are key signatures in a wide range of heavy-flavor and exotic decay processes. The LHCb Run 3 detector now operates with a fully software-based trigger that processes the complete detector readout at the LHC bunch-crossing rate, with the first trigger stage executed on GPUs. In this environment, particle-identification algorithms must achieve high efficiency and background rejection while satisfying stringent constraints on throughput and memory footprint. We present algorithms for muon and electron identification in the LHCb Run 3 GPU trigger based on Lipschitz-constrained neural networks. Separate networks are developed for muons and electrons and are trained using simulated events. Their performance is evaluated relative to the previous baseline algorithms, demonstrating improved discrimination across a wide range of kinematic regions while remaining compatible with the requirements of real-time GPU execution.

Method: 采用CASPEr-electric方案：在极化铁电晶体中操控^{207}Pb核自旋系综，利用SQUID探测ALP背景场通过g_d耦合产生的振荡扭矩；通过扫磁场调谐至对应axion Compton频率（4.6–5.0 MHz与5.2–5.3 MHz），并以脉冲磁共振进行小倾角校准。

Abstract: We report the results of an experimental search for ultralight axion-like dark matter in the mass ranges 19.5--20.5 and 21.5--22 neV. The Cosmic Axion Spin Precession Experiment-electric probes the axion-nuclear electric dipole moment coupling. We perform solid-state nuclear magnetic resonance on $^{207}$Pb spin ensembles which reside in a polarized ferroelectric crystal. The background axion-like dark matter field induces an oscillating torque on the $^{207}$Pb spins via the electric dipole moment coupling $g_{d}$. The experiment is calibrated with pulsed magnetic resonance measurements covering two frequency bands (4.6--5.0 and 5.2--5.3 MHz) that correspond to the axion Compton frequencies associated with the identified axion mass ranges. By sweeping the leading magnetic field applied to the sample, we are able to detect the $^{207}$Pb nuclear magnetic resonance in these frequency ranges using a superconducting quantum interference device, inductively coupled to the sample with a broadband circuit. We establish the upper bounds $|g_{d}| < 4\times 10^{-4}$ GeV$^{-2}$ with 95% confidence in these frequency bands. Our results demonstrate the detection and small-tip angle calibration of low-field $^{207}$Pb nuclear magnetic resonance, allowing for sensitivity to axion-like dark matter in the nanoelectron-volt axion mass range.

*Jiwon Lee,Jinsu Kim,Ohjoon Kwon,Saebyeok Ahn,Seonjeong Oh,Sergey Uchaikin,Boris Ivanov,Arjan Ferdinand van Loo,Yasunobu Nakamura,Dojun Youm,SungWoo Youn,HyoungSoon Choi*

TL;DR: 本文利用高Q值高温超导（HTS）轴子暗物质探测器，在15.30–15.85 μeV质量区间对轴子-光子耦合强度进行了搜寻，获得Frequentist 90% CL排除限低至1.3×g_{aγ}^{KSVZ}，Bayesian分析达KSVZ基准灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴子作为冷暗物质候选者，其参数空间在微电子伏特质量区仍待实验覆盖；传统铜腔受限于品质因数Q，需发展更高性能谐振腔以提升探测灵敏度。

Method: 构建基于REBCO高温超导薄膜（剥离衬底后焊接到铜壳上）的可调谐HTS谐振腔，在8 T强磁场中运行；采用Frequentist与Bayesian双统计框架分析数据。

Result: 在15.30–15.85 μeV质量区间实现最高Q因子达铜腔3–4倍；Frequentist 90% CL排除极限为g_{aγ} ≤ 1.3 × g_{aγ}^{KSVZ}；Bayesian后验灵敏度达g_{aγ} = g_{aγ}^{KSVZ}。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在μeV轴子窗口实现HTS谐振腔主导的突破性灵敏度，验证了REBCO薄膜腔在轴子搜寻中的可行性，为下一代宽频带高Q haloscope提供关键技术路径。

Abstract: We report an axion dark matter search in the mass range of $15.30\text{--}15.85\,μ\text{eV}$ using a broadly tunable high-temperature superconducting (HTS) haloscope. By soldering substrate-stripped rare-earth barium copper oxide (REBCO) films onto a copper shell, we achieved a quality factor 3--4 times higher than in copper cavities across its tuning band in an 8\text{-T} magnetic field. We set robust Frequentist 90\%~confidence-level exclusion limits on the axion--photon coupling down to 1.3 times the KSVZ coupling, while a complementary Bayesian analysis achieves KSVZ-level sensitivity.

</details>

J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用BESIII实验采集的20.3 fb⁻¹数据，首次搜索了D⁰→K⁺π⁻η′和D⁺→K⁺π⁰η′两类双卡比博压低（DCSD）衰变，未观测到显著信号，给出了分支比90%置信度上限，并据此导出相对于卡比博允许衰变的比值上限，以tan⁴θ_C为标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验标准模型中CKM矩阵元的幺正性与夸克味改变弱相互作用的结构，特别是通过双卡比博压低衰变探查可能的新物理效应或强子动力学修正。

Method: 基于BESIII在√s = 3.773 GeV下采集的e⁺e⁻对撞数据（∫ℒ = 20.3 fb⁻¹），采用事件计数法结合蒙特卡洛模拟确定探测效率与背景分布，使用似然拟合或侧带方法估计本底，并依据CLₛ方法计算90%置信度上限。

Result: D⁰→K⁺π⁻η′与D⁺→K⁺π⁰η′的分支比上限分别为3.0×10⁻⁵与2.1×10⁻⁵（90% CL）；相应DCSD/CF比值上限为1.6 tan⁴θ_C与3.7 tan⁴θ_C。

Conclusion: 当前精度下未发现显著DCSD信号，所设上限与标准模型预期一致，为未来高统计量实验（如BESIII升级或Super Tau-Charm Factory）提供了基准，并约束了非微扰强子矩阵元及可能的SU(3)破缺效应。

Abstract: We present the first search for the doubly Cabibbo-suppressed decays $D^0\to K^+π^-η^\prime$ and $D^+\to K^+π^0η^\prime$ using an $e^+e^-$ collision data sample corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 20.3 fb$^{-1}$, collected at a center-of-mass energy of 3.773 GeV with the Beijing Spectrometer III (BESIII) detector at the Beijing Electron-Positron Collider II (BEPCII). No significant signals are observed, and the upper limits on their decay branching fractions are set to be $3.0\times 10^{-5}$ and $2.1\times 10^{-5}$ at the 90% confidence level, respectively. By combining these results with the world-average branching fractions of the corresponding Cabibbo-favored decays, upper limits at the 90% confidence level are obtained on the ratios of doubly Cabibbo-suppressed to Cabibbo-favored branching fractions. The limits are determined to be $1.6\times \tan^4θ_C$ and $3.7\times \tan^4θ_C$ for $D^0\to K^+π^-η^\prime$ and $D^+\to K^+π^0η^\prime$, respectively, where $θ_C$ denotes the Cabibbo mixing angle.

</details>


### [71] [Prospects for precision Higgs boson measurements at a 10 TeV muon collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08392)
*Paolo Andreetto,Massimo Casarsa,Luca Castelli,Alessio Gianelle,Donatella Lucchesi,Leonardo Palombini,Imran Raghib,Lorenzo Sestini,Davide Zuliani*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文综述了10 TeV缪子对撞机在希格斯物理精密测量中的潜力，重点评估单/双希格斯产生截面、希格斯质量及三线性自耦合的探测灵敏度，基于MUSIC探测器详细模拟与机器本底建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升希格斯扇区参数（尤其是三线性自耦合）的测量精度，检验标准模型并探索新物理；缪子对撞机因高亮度、低轫致辐射损失及强矢量玻色子融合产率，成为优于电子对撞机的理想平台。

Method: 采用MUSIC探测器的详细蒙特卡洛模拟（含机器诱导本底），针对两个基准实验配置（各10 ab⁻¹积分亮度），分析H→bb̄, WW∗, γγ, ZZ∗, μ⁺μ⁻等衰变道的统计精度，并组合通道以提升希格斯质量与λ₃测量精度。

Result: H→bb̄与WW∗截面统计精度达0.18%与0.35%；H→γγ, ZZ∗, μ⁺μ⁻分别为2.6%、4.2%、6.9%；组合质量测量精度~19 MeV；HH→bbbb与bbWW∗双希格斯截面精度为4.2%与14%；由HH→bbbb可得三线性耦合λ₃精度~5%。

Conclusion: 10 TeV缪子对撞机具备实现亚百分比级希格斯参数测量的能力，尤其在三线性自耦合测量上达到~5%精度，确立其作为下一代希格斯工厂的核心地位。

Abstract: A 10 TeV muon collider offers a powerful environment for precision studies of the Higgs sector through its large vector-boson-fusion production rates. This review presents projected sensitivities to single- and double-Higgs production cross sections, the Higgs boson mass, and the trilinear Higgs self-coupling. The projections are obtained from detailed simulations of the MUSIC detector, including machine-induced background, for a baseline configuration of two experiments, each collecting an integrated luminosity of 10 $\mathrm{ab}^{-1}$. The $H\to b\bar{b}$ and $H\to WW^\ast$ production cross sections can be measured with statistical precisions of 0.18% and 0.35%, respectively, while precisions of 2.6%, 4.2%, and 6.9% are expected for $H\toγγ$, $H\to ZZ^\ast$, and $H\toμ^+μ^-$. Combining the $H\to b\bar{b}$, $H\toγγ$, and $H\toμ^+μ^-$ channels yields an expected Higgs boson mass precision of about 19 MeV. Double-Higgs production can be measured with statistical precisions of 4.2% in the $HH\to b\bar{b}b\bar{b}$ channel and 14% in the $HH\to b\bar{b}WW^\ast$ channel. Using the $HH\to b\bar{b}b\bar{b}$ channel, the trilinear Higgs self-coupling can be determined with an expected precision of about 5%. These results demonstrate the potential of a high-energy muon collider for precision Higgs physics.

</details>


### [72] [Readout electronics for SUBMET](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08427)
*Claudio Campagnari,Sungwoong Cho,Suyong Choi,Seokju Chung,Matthew Citron,Albert De Roeck,Martin Gastal,Seungkyu Ha,Andy Haas,Christopher Scott Hill,Insung Hwang,Hoyong Jeong,Jaebak Kim,Jeonghwa Kim,Hyunki Moon,Ryan Schmitz,David Stuart,Eunil Won,Jae Hyeok Yoo,Jinseok Yoo,Ayman Youssef,Ahmad Zaraket,Haitham Zaraket*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种专为SUBMET实验设计的高精度数据采集（DAQ）系统，用于探测电荷量低于10^{-3}e的毫微电荷粒子（mCPs），系统基于DRS4芯片实现单光电子级灵敏度、亚纳秒级通道间时间同步及低噪声前端电子学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测电荷量 Q = εe（ε < O(10^{-3})）的毫微电荷粒子（mCPs），其信号极弱（仅数个闪烁光子），需极高灵敏度与时间分辨能力的DAQ系统。

Method: 采用级联DRS4芯片（每组4通道，共2组输入），以820.5 MHz采样率连续采集4096点/通道，覆盖5 μs时间窗；通过硬件校准实现通道间时间同步（片内<1 ns，板内<2 ns，跨板<8 ns）；前端电路RMS噪声<0.4 mV，并引入正向基线偏置以优化负脉冲电压分辨率与动态范围；由触发控制板统一聚合多板数据并维持束流运行所需吞吐率。

Result: 系统实测满足SUBMET实验要求：时间同步精度优于30 ns符合窗，单光电子探测能力确立，噪声水平达0.4 mV RMS，数据吞吐率适配J-PARC 30 GeV质子束八脉冲结构。

Conclusion: 该DAQ系统成功实现了面向超弱光信号、亚纳秒时间关联与高通量束流环境的定制化设计，为SUBMET实验中mCPs的灵敏搜寻提供了可靠技术支撑。

Abstract: A dedicated data acquisition (DAQ) system has been developed for the SUB-Millicharge ExperimenT (SUBMET) at the Japan Proton Accelerator Research Complex (J-PARC), a search for particles carrying a fractional electric charge $Q = εe$ with $ε$ below $\mathcal{O}(10^{-3})$, hereafter referred to as millicharged particles (mCPs). Because such particles are expected to produce at most a few scintillation photons, the system is optimized for single-photoelectron detection from the photomultiplier tubes (PMTs), combining high-speed waveform digitization with precise timing. To capture eight consecutive proton bunches of the 30 GeV J-PARC beam within a single trigger, the eight channels of the Domino Ring Sampler 4 (DRS4) chip are cascaded in groups of four to form two readout inputs, each sampling 4096 points continuously at 820.5 MHz over an effective time window of 5 us. After calibration, timing differences between channels are within 1 ns on the same DRS4 chip, 2 ns on the same board, and 8 ns across different boards, well within the 30 ns coincidence window of the experiment. The front-end electronics achieve an RMS noise below 0.4 mV. The baseline is deliberately offset upward such that the negative-going pulses span a larger fraction of the digitizer range, improving voltage resolution and dynamic range. A trigger control board aggregates data from multiple readout boards and sustains the data-transfer rate required for beam operation. The measured performance confirms that the DAQ system meets the timing, noise, and throughput requirements of the experiment.

</details>

Abstract: Geant4 is a toolkit for simulating particle interactions in matter that is widely used in particle physics, nuclear physics, medical imaging, and astrophysics. At present, Geant4 visualizations are generally viewed on a conventional, two-dimensional computer display. To enhance immersion, we have developed G4VR, a virtual reality (VR) application that extends Geant4 with VR capabilities, providing a more intuitive and comprehensive way to understand these interactions. The user running G4VR in a headset can get detailed information about individual tracks, interactions, and energy depositions by selecting them with the hand controllers. G4VR also allows the user to examine the chronology of interactions, and includes animations of entire events as immersive movies. G4VR includes built-in event displays of several types that allow a first-time user to easily explore its various functionalities. To interface with Geant4, we have developed a new visualization driver called G4XR that allows direct integration with the Geant4 source code. This enables any Geant4 experiment to be loaded and experienced in VR seamlessly.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决标准模型味物理、重子不对称起源及中微子质量三者之间的深层联系问题，即'味-重子-中微子统一性'；同时规避传统重子生成机制（如电弱重子生成或轻子生成）在能标与CP源上的困难。

Method: 构建含全局U(1)_H对称性的最小Froggatt-Nielsen模型，引入轴子型伪戈德斯通玻色子；耦合Weinberg维度五算符以实现B−L破坏；求解包含SM粒子数密度、手征不对称及轴子场演化的完整Boltzmann方程组；严格计入轴子衰变导致的熵稀释与重子等曲率扰动约束。

Result: 该机制可在T_osc > 10^11 GeV、f_a ∈ [10^13–10^14, >10^17] GeV参数区间内复现观测BAU（η_B ≈ 6×10^{−10}）；轴子运动诱导的自发CPT破坏为重子生成提供必要CP源；BAU大小可跨越数个数量级，且天然与轻中微子质量尺度相关。

Conclusion: 最小Froggatt-Nielsen框架在引入全局水平对称性破缺后，不仅解释味结构，还可作为自洽的重子生成机制，构成‘味起源→轴子动力学→CPT破坏→B−L弛豫→BAU’的因果链；该机制具有可观测预言（如超重轴子、特定能标下的等曲率扰动抑制），并可推广至其他全局水平对称群（如U(1)^n、离散群）及广义轴子初值分布。

Abstract: In its minimal realization, the Froggatt-Nielsen (FN) mechanism addresses the Standard Model (SM) flavor puzzle via the spontaneous breaking of a horizontal $U(1)_H$ symmetry. If $U(1)_H$ is global and broken before the end of inflation, it gives rise to a pseudo-Nambu-Goldstone boson (axion-like particle) with a homogeneous initial misalignment, whose subsequent coherent evolution sources spontaneous $CPT$ violation in the SM sector. We show that in the presence of $B-L$-violating processes $-$ parametrized here model-independently via the dimension-five Weinberg operator responsible for light neutrino masses $-$ the minimal FN setup can generate the baryon asymmetry of the Universe (BAU), linking the origin of flavor directly to the BAU and neutrino masses. Solving a complete set of evolution equations for SM asymmetries, we demonstrate that this mechanism can readily yield a baryon asymmetry comparable to the observed value, or even exceeding it by several orders of magnitude. Accounting for baryonic isocurvature and entropy dilution from axion decays, we find that the observed BAU is successfully reproduced for axion oscillation temperatures above $10^{11}$ GeV and axion decay constants ranging from $10^{13\cdots 14}$ GeV to above $10^{17}$ GeV. Finally, while we focus on a global $U(1)_H$ symmetry and axion motion from standard misalignment, our framework is readily extendable to other global horizontal symmetries and arbitrary axion motion.

</details>

Method: 联合分析包容喷注R_AA与小角度区域的能量-能量关联器（EEC），采用数据驱动方法提取夸克/胶子淬火因子Q_q/Q_g及介质分辨尺度；以相干能量损失为零假设，对比引入天线能量损失（antenna energy loss）模型的拟合能力。

Conclusion: QGP介质具有有限分辨能力，可在动量空间部分解析喷注内部色相干结构；色退相干是喷注淬火中不可忽略的物理机制，其存在为QGP作为强耦合色介质提供了关键实验证据。

Abstract: We combine inclusive jet nuclear modification factors with energy-energy correlators to perform a data-driven extraction of the quark and gluon quenching factors and the medium resolution scale from the small-angle region. Taking coherent energy loss as a null hypothesis, we find jet quenching factors incompatible with Casimir scaling. Incorporating color decoherence through antenna energy loss improves the description of the data and restores Casimir scaling, providing evidence that the quark-gluon plasma partially resolves the internal structure of jets in heavy-ion collisions.

</details>

Abstract: Using jet calculus at leading-logarithmic accuracy, we develop a generating-functional approach to describe the hard-collinear sector of jets propagating through the quark-gluon plasma. This framework yields coupled evolution equations for the jet nuclear modification factor and energy-energy correlators (EECs). We first construct the EEC evolution in the fully incoherent regime, and then derive evolution equations that account for the transition from the coherent to the incoherent limit in the large-$N_c$ limit. Furthermore, we show that combining the EEC with the jet nuclear modification factor provides complementary sensitivity to the overall jet suppression and to medium-induced modifications of the jet's internal structure. This framework therefore offers a way to constrain the magnitude and flavor dependence of jet energy loss, together with the medium resolution angle governing color decoherence.

We show that the improved setup can determine the dark matter mass with high accuracy, significantly expand the detectable mass range, and achieve the best sensitivity to date in the $8\times10^{-14}\sim1\times10^{-12}{\rm~ eV}$ mass range.

### [79] [Probing the $B^{0}$ meson in a hot medium with finite baryon chemical potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08299)
*A. Türkan,G. Bozkır,K. Azizi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用QCD两点半径求和规则，定量研究了B⁰介子在高温高密介质中的质量与衰变常数随温度T和重子化学势μ_B的变化行为，发现二者均呈现先增后减并最终消失的非单调依赖关系，且消失温度/化学势随另一参数增大而降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强相互作用物质在极端条件（如重离子碰撞或中子星内部）下的介子性质演化，特别是B⁰介子作为含底夸克探针在QCD相结构与手征/退禁闭动力学中的敏感性。

Abstract: We construct a matrix model to study the deconfining phase transition for a pure gluon plasma that is confined in a cylinder of radius ${\cal R}$ and rotating rigidly at a real-valued angular velocity $Ω$, satisfying $\mathcal{R} Ω<1$. The deconfining phase transition arises due to the competition between two terms that constitute the matrix model. The perturbative term comes from the one-loop effective potential computed in the presence of a background field, while the non-perturbative term represents a correction to the perturbative contribution which is brought about by taking into account an effective mass of the gauge fields. Our results show that real rotation induces a radial inhomogeneity of the system and the deconfining temperature $T_c$ drops away from the rotation axis which is consistent with the Tolman-Ehrenfest law. As for the $Ω$-dependence of $T_c$, it relies on our assumptions of the gluon effective mass. For a constant mass, $T_c$ is found to always decrease with increasing $Ω$. A non-monotonic behavior of $T_c$ shows up when a $Ω$-dependent mass is considered, leading to a qualitative change in the region of small angular velocity. In addition, by setting $Ω=0$ to eliminate rotational effects, we also demonstrate that the finite-volume effect reduces the deconfining temperature relative to the infinite-volume limit. Comparisons between our results and those from various lattice simulations and phenomenological models suggest that controversy remains over how the deconfining phase transition is modified by real rotation and further work is required to reach a definite conclusion.

One event then requires σ_p\simeq2\times10^{-42} cm^2 and a kinetic mixing ε\simeq1\times10^{-6} at m_{A'}=1\GeV, with beam-dump coverage below 0.6\GeV pushing the mediator above that value. Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于A4模对称性的新框架，统一解释狄拉克中微子质量起源与自相互作用暗物质（SIDM）性质；通过狄拉克逆向跷跷板机制生成中微子质量，引入单态标量φ作为暗物质质量劈裂源与轻中介子，实现伪狄拉克、非弹性散射及小尺度结构问题缓解，并自然解释LZ230616事件；暗宇称破缺导致畴壁网络，其显式破缺产生可探测的随机引力波背景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 构建基于非全纯A₄模对称性的有效场论模型：引入单态标量φ获得狄拉克逆向跷跷板中微子质量；φ同时耦合矢量型费米子暗物质，诱导微小马约拉纳质量劈裂，形成伪狄拉克、非弹性暗物质；φ作为轻中介子介导暗物质自相互作用；暗宇称自发破缺生成畴壁，显式破缺驱动畴壁湮灭并辐射随机引力波。

Result: 模型参数空间自然容纳LZ230616 248 keV事例（通过端吸散射运动学）；预测可检验的非高斯随机引力波背景（峰值频率~mHz–Hz）；中微子振荡参数、ΔN_eff、SIDM截面与GW信号由模函数约束而强关联；暗物质为伪狄拉克、热退耦合、自相互作用截面满足σ/m ∼ 1 cm²/g。

Conclusion: 该A₄模对称性框架首次将狄拉克中微子质量机制、非弹性SIDM、LZ异常信号、畴壁宇宙学与随机引力波背景统一于单一理论结构，兼具预测性、简约性与可检验性。

Abstract: We propose a novel framework that simultaneously addresses the origin of Dirac neutrino masses and the nature of self-interacting dark matter (SIDM). The model is based on an $A_{4}$ modular symmetry to ensure the Diracness of neutrinos as well as the stability of the DM. The neutrino sector realizes a Dirac Inverse Seesaw mechanism where the smallness of the neutrino mass is governed by the vacuum expectation value (VEV) of a singlet scalar $φ$. This same scalar couples to a vector-like fermion DM candidate, inducing a tiny Majorana mass splitting that renders the DM pseudo-Dirac and inelastic. Crucially, the scalar also acts as a light mediator for DM self-interactions, potentially solving the small-scale structure problems of Cold DM. In light of the recent 248 keV nuclear-recoil event, LZ230616, observed by LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) DM direct detection experiment, we demonstrate that our inelastic SIDM parameter space naturally accommodates this signal via endothermic scattering kinematics. Furthermore, the spontaneous breaking of the dark parity required for this inelasticity produces a network of cosmological domain walls. We show that the explicit symmetry breaking needed to safely annihilate these walls generates a stochastic gravitational wave background. The non-holomorphic modular symmetry reduces the free parameters, correlating neutrino observables, addressing DM phenomenology, $ΔN_{\rm eff}$, and gravitational-wave signatures.

</details>


### [96] [Inverse seesaw and inflation in a supersymmetric 331 model from $SU(6)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06734)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建自洽的大统一框架，将中微子质量起源（逆跷跷板）、超对称破缺传播机制、规范耦合统一、电弱对称性破缺及暴胀动力学在单一超对称SU(6)结构下统一描述，避免人为引入独立能标与参数。

Method: 采用N=1超对称SU(6)群论嵌套结构，构造包含SU(3)_C×SU(3)_L×U(1)_X中间对称性的对称性破缺链；利用反对称狄拉克矩阵的代数性质分析中微子质量谱；通过超势中spurion场与信使耦合确定SUSY破缺传递与轻子数破坏尺度；结合RGE演化与阈值修正分析规范耦合统一性；在中性标量子空间(Φ,\barΦ,S)中检验暴胀势稳定性与径向质量。

Conclusion: 该SU(6)模型成功整合中微子物理、超对称规范介导、大统一与暴胀，所有关键能标由少数耦合与对称性破缺模式决定，无需额外自由参数，为高能物理与宇宙学提供统一理论候选。

Abstract: We propose a $N=1$ supersymmetric $SU(6)$ theory with an intermediate $(SU(3)_C\times SU(3)_L\times U(1)_X)$ phase, in which common fields and operators relate the neutrinos, gauge mediation, unification, and symmetry-breaking sectors. The inverse-seesaw Dirac matrix is antisymmetric and thus has rank two, leaving one massless neutrino at leading order. The leading symmetry-allowed correction lifts this zero mode through its projection onto the left and right null vectors of the Dirac matrix, resulting in a quartic suppression by the ratio of the intermediate and ultraviolet scales. The complete unified messenger multiplets preserve the relative one-loop gauge-coupling crossing, while the supersymmetry-breaking spurion entering the messenger sector also determines the scale of lepton-number violation. The coupling governing (331) breaking fixes both the inflationary normalization and the radial mass in the broken vacuum. For the conditional heavy spectrum adopted in the threshold calculations, the gauge couplings remain perturbative. The physical scalar directions transverse to the inflaton are stable within the neutral $((Φ,\barΦ,S))$ subspace. These results provide the analytical scale relations among the inverse seesaw, messenger sector, unified gauge evolution, and inflation, while keeping the assumptions associated with the ultraviolet Higgs and heavy-threshold sectors explicitly.

</details>


### [97] [High-Energy Nuclear Recoils from Boosted Dark Matter for the LZ 248-keV Event: Beyond the Halo-Dependent High-Velocity Tail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06890)
*Haider Alhazmi,Doojin Kim,Kyoungchul Kong,Jong-Chul Park,Seodong Shin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: LZ实验观测到一个248 keV核反冲事例，显著性为3.4σ（局部）/2.6σ（全局）；本文提出轻型加速暗物质（BDM）解释，强调其能谱由暗区质量谱决定，通过近阈值吸热散射与暗区不可见衰变实现单核反冲信号，并给出可检验的基准谱与互补探测方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LZ实验中观测到的孤立高能核反冲事例（248 keV），规避对银河系高速尾部速度分布这一不确定天体物理输入的强依赖。

Method: 构建多组分轻型加速暗物质（BDM）模型，包含弹性/非弹性核散射机制；引入赝标量媒介子导致动量压制的低能截断；分析近阈值endothermic散射产生的运动学确定的双端点 recoil 谱；考虑上散射态在暗区内的不可见衰变以维持单核反冲事例特征。

Result: 提出一类BDM模型可自然再现LZ事例的能量、统计显著性及单事例特征；导出具有清晰低/高能截断的 recoil 谱；给出可在Xe、Ar、Ge等靶核及大型液闪实验（如JUNO、Hyper-K）中验证的可观测量。

Conclusion: 该BDM解释提供了一种不依赖银河速度分布先验、且与现有直接探测约束兼容的新机制；其预测的 recoil 谱形状与多靶核响应为未来实验提供了可证伪的关键检验。

Abstract: The LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) Collaboration has reported a nuclear-recoil candidate at $E_R=248\pm23_{\rm stat}\pm23_{\rm sys}$ keV, with a maximum local significance of $3.4σ$ and a global significance of $2.6σ$. A prominent interpretation invokes heavy halo dark matter near an inelastic threshold and therefore depends sensitively on the poorly constrained high-speed tail of the Galactic velocity distribution. In this Letter, we propose a qualitatively different possibility based on light boosted dark matter (BDM), whose incident energy is determined primarily by the dark-sector mass spectrum. We consider multi-component scenarios in which the boosted state scatters elastically or inelastically off xenon nuclei. For elastic scattering, pseudoscalar-mediated momentum dependence suppresses low-energy recoils. Near-threshold endothermic scattering of a nearly monochromatic BDM flux can instead confine the signal between kinematically determined recoil endpoints, suppressing events in both the low- and high-energy sidebands. The upscattered state may furthermore decay invisibly within the dark sector, preserving a single-nuclear-recoil signature without requiring it to be detector-stable. We present representative benchmark spectra and discuss complementary tests using other target nuclei and large-volume liquid-scintillator experiments.

</details>


### [98] [GUT-induced FCC signatures of the LUX-ZEPLIN event](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06750)
*Wojciech Kotlarski,Kamila Kowalska,Enrico Maria Sessolo*

TL;DR: 本文探讨了LUX-ZEPLIN实验中观测到的大反冲能事件，提出伪狄拉克暗物质（如MSSM中的希格斯ino）的非弹性散射可作为解释，并指出在GUT诱导模型（基于SU(6)）中，该现象所要求的质量劈裂δm自然对应一个10 TeV量级的Z′玻色子；该Z′可在FCC-hh对撞机的双轻子道中被早期发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LUX-ZEPLIN实验中观测到的大反冲能事例，并检验伪狄拉克暗物质（特别是MSSM希格斯ino）在GUT框架下是否具有可观测的高能对撞信号。

Method: 基于SU(6)大统一理论构造具体模型，关联伪狄拉克质量劈裂δm与重粒子（特别是Z′规范玻色子）质量；计算Z′在FCC-hh上的 dilepton共振截面，并与探测器灵敏度比较。

Result: 实验偏好的δm ≈ 100–200 keV对应Z′质量为O(10) TeV；该Z′在FCC-hh的pp → Z′ → ℓ⁺ℓ⁻过程中具有显著产额，其信号远超早期运行阶段的探测阈值。

Conclusion: 伪狄拉克暗物质的地下直接探测信号与未来对撞机Z′物理存在强协同性；FCC-hh有望在早期运行中发现该GUT预言的Z′，从而为MSSM希格斯ino暗物质提供关键验证。

Abstract: The recent observation of an event with large recoil energy at LUX-ZEPLIN has found a possible explanation in terms of the inelastic scattering of a pseudo-Dirac particle like the Higgsino of the MSSM. For pseudo-Dirac dark matter, the mass splitting $δm$ is correlated with the mass of some heavier states. While rough dimensional considerations seem to indicate mass ranges of the heavy particles hopelessly out of reach even at future colliders, we point out that this is not the case in GUT-induced scenarios. Working with a specific model based on SU(6), we show that the experimentally favored $δm$ implies the presence of a $Z'$ gauge boson with a mass in the 10s of TeV. We derive the predicted $Z'$ signal in the dilepton channel and we confront it with the sensitivity of the FCC-$hh$ to $Z'$ resonances. We show that the $Z'$ falls squarely in reach of the early discovery potential of the FCC-$hh$.

</details>

Method: 构建基于规范U(1)_{B-L}对称性的两分量标量暗物质模型（含实标量S及其激发态S*），引入质量劈裂Δm ∼ keV量级以实现非弹性散射；采用共振湮灭机制（S S → Z′* → SM）计算热退耦丰度，并结合LZ探测阈值与核反冲谱拟合确定参数空间。

Result: 获得与LZ事例兼容的参数点：m_S ≃ m_{Z′}/2 ≈ O(1) TeV，g_{B-L} ≈ 0.5，非弹性自旋无关截面σ_SI ≈ 1.2 × 10⁻⁴⁵ cm²，且满足Planck测得的Ωh² ≈ 0.12。

Conclusion: 该U(1)_{B-L}标量暗物质模型不仅自然复现LZ异常事例，同时满足宇宙学丰度与直接探测约束；Z′玻色子在LHC或未来高能对撞机上的共振产生将成为该模型的关键可检验预言。

Abstract: We show that our previously proposed inelastic scalar dark matter (DM) model in gauged $U(1)_{B-L}$ symmetry naturally accounts for the $2.6σ$ high-energy recoil event recently reported by the LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) collaboration. To satisfy the thermal relic abundance, TeV-scale scalar DM $S$ with $m_S \simeq m_{Z'}/2$ for resonant annihilation and the gauge coupling constant $g_{B-L} \sim 0.5$ are required, where $m_{Z^\prime}$ is the mass of $U(1)_{B-L}$ gauge boson $Z^{\prime}$. The resulting predicted DM-nucleon inelastic cross section of $σ_\mathrm{SI} \sim 10^{-45}$ cm${}^2$ is in excellent agreement with the LZ event. This scenario can be decisively tested for future collider searches for $Z^\prime$ boson resonance.

</details>


### [100] [LZ Nuclear-Recoil Excess from Boosted Light Magnetic Dipole-dipole Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06756)

TL;DR: LZ实验观测到248 keV附近的核反冲事例超出（2.6σ），标准晕暗物质磁偶极相互作用模型虽能拟合该超出，但与低能区无超出相冲突；本文提出 boosted速度分布可缓解该张力，并允许GeV量级轻暗物质解释该信号，且在含暗区中介子衰变的具体模型中得以实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调和LZ实验在248 keV处的核反冲超出与低能区无显著超出之间的张力，探索非标准暗物质速度分布（特别是boosted分布）对事例谱形状的影响。

Method: 采用模型无关的参数化方法分析boosted速度分布对核反冲能谱的修正；进而构建具体粒子物理模型：晕暗物质通过湮灭产生静止质量非零的暗区中介子，其衰变产生具有高boost因子的子暗物质粒子，从而获得窄峰状、高动能的反冲谱。

Result: boosted速度分布显著改善对LZ能谱的整体拟合，降低低能区过预测；允许暗物质质量低至~GeV量级（远低于TeV），同时保持对248 keV超出的良好拟合；该机制在具体暗区模型中自洽实现。

Conclusion: boosted暗区粒子是LZ反冲超出的一个可行物理解释，它突破了传统晕暗物质质量下限，凸显了速度分布非标准性在直接探测数据分析中的关键作用。

Abstract: The LZ collaboration has reported a nuclear-recoil excess near 248 keV with a global significance of $2.6σ$. Although halo dark matter with a magnetic dipole-dipole interaction and a TeV-scale mass provides the best fit to the excess among the interactions considered by LZ, it predicts a considerable number of events at lower recoil energies, where no excess is observed. We show that a boosted velocity distribution can alleviate this tension and provide a better fit to the LZ recoil spectrum. Moreover, the boost opens up the possibility of explaining the excess with much lighter dark matter, with masses down to the GeV scale. We demonstrate these features first in a model-independent analysis and then realize them in a concrete dark matter model, in which halo dark matter annihilates into on-shell mediators that subsequently decay into boosted dark-sector particles. Our results demonstrate that boosted dark sector particles provide a viable interpretation of the LZ excess.

</details>


### [101] [LZ nuclear recoil event from inelastic singlet-doublet scalar dark matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07451)

Abstract: We study the possibility of explaining the recently reported high-energy nuclear recoil event by the LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) collaboration within the framework of singlet-doublet scalar dark matter (DM). Considering DM to be the CP even mass eigenstate formed out of a scalar doublet and a real scalar singlet, both being odd under an unbroken $Z_2$ symmetry, we find the parameter space of the model consistent with correct relic abundance and direct-detection limits on elastic scattering rates. A large part of the parameter space can also lead to inelastic up-scattering of DM with a rate and recoil energy consistent with the recent LZ event. Depending upon the singlet-doublet mixing, the model allows a much wider range of currently allowed parameter space compared to the pure scalar doublet DM limit. Presence of $Z_2$-odd right-handed neutrinos also leads to other interesting phenomenology related to the origin of light neutrino masses and leptogenesis.

</details>


### [102] [Solar Capture Tests of Inelastic Dark Matter after the LZ High-Recoil Event](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06760)

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ实验观测到一个248 keV核反冲候选事例，endothermic暗物质可给出较高局部显著性；需检验其是否与太阳内暗物质捕获-湮灭产生的中微子通量（IceCube限制）相容。

Method: 采用太阳内暗物质捕获率C⊙计算、两态动力学建模（含热辅助再激发χ₁→χ₂）、有限温度俘获修正、自旋响应不确定性分析，并以IceCube b̄b中微子谱为基准强子末态模板进行湮灭率Γ_A上限比对。

Result: 1) 热Higgsino：δ≈0.38 MeV分裂被IceCube要求δ≳0.51–0.56 MeV排除；2) 伪Dirac费米子：C⊙=1.48×10²⁰ s⁻¹，但两态抑制使Γ_A最大仅≈4.1×10¹⁴ s⁻¹，比IceCube b̄b极限低约1.8×10⁵；3) O₄中子亲和模型（mχ=1 TeV, δ=300 keV）：C⊙=3.98×10¹⁷ s⁻¹，平衡态Γ_A上限仍比b̄b极限低因子~154（激进假设下仍低因子~19）。

Conclusion: Solar capture via IceCube neutrino limits robustly excludes the canonical thermal Higgsino interpretation of the LZ candidate, but does not generically rule out endothermic DM explanations—particularly pseudo-Dirac and neutron-philic O₄ scenarios—due to suppressed solar annihilation from kinematic or dynamical effects.

Abstract: The LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) Collaboration has reported a $248$ keV nuclear-recoil candidate for which endothermic dark matter (DM) gives some of the largest local significances. We study Solar-capture constraints on three simple interpretations: a thermal Higgsino, a thermal pseudo-Dirac fermion with off-diagonal vector interactions, and neutron-philic endothermic spin-dependent scattering through $\mathcal{O}_4$. The canonical full-density thermal Higgsino is excluded: its LZ-preferred splitting near $0.38,\mathrm{MeV}$ lies below the IceCube requirement, $δ\gtrsim0.51$--$0.56,\mathrm{MeV}$. For the pseudo-Dirac benchmark we find $C_\odot=1.48\times10^{20},\mathrm{s}^{-1}$, but two-state kinetics strongly suppresses annihilation. Including thermally assisted $χ_1\toχ_2$ re-excitation gives a maximum $Γ_A\simeq4.1\times10^{14},\mathrm{s}^{-1}$, about $1.8\times10^5$ below the adopted IceCube $b\bar b$ limit. For neutron-philic $\mathcal{O}_4$ scattering at $m_χ=1$ TeV and $δ=300$ keV, finite-temperature capture gives $C_\odot=3.98\times10^{17},\mathrm{s}^{-1}$, with spin-response range $(1.77$--$9.72)\times10^{17},\mathrm{s}^{-1}$. Even in equilibrium, the upper edge remains about a factor $154$ below the adopted $b\bar b$ limit, while an aggressive no-finite-$q$ suppression plus upper-spin stress test still leaves a factor about $19$. The $b\bar b$ mapping is used as a representative hadronic template for the two non-Higgsino benchmarks. Solar capture therefore excludes the thermal-Higgsino interpretation but does not generically exclude endothermic explanations of the LZ candidate.

</details>


### [103] [Inelastic Singlet-Doublet Fermion Dark Matter in light of the 248 keV LZ event](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07800)
*Debasish Borah,Sujit Kumar Sahoo,Narendra Sahu,Shashwat Sharma*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: LZ实验观测到一个248 keV核反冲事例，暗示暗物质-原子核非弹性散射；本文研究单-二重态费米子暗物质模型，其中轻质量态为马约拉纳费米子，禁戒树图Z玻色子介导的弹性散射，但允许Z交换的非弹性散射，并引入Z₂偶极标量三重态实现伪狄拉克劈裂与II型跷跷板中微子质量机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LZ实验在248 keV处观测到的孤立核反冲事例及低能区事例缺失——该现象天然契合非弹性暗物质-原子核散射机制。

Method: 构建扩展的singlet-doublet fermion DM模型：包含一对近简并的伪狄拉克费米子（轻者为马约拉纳态），其Z玻色子耦合满足手征抑制弹性散射但允许激发态跃迁的非弹性散射；引入Z₂-偶标量三重态以同时生成质量劈裂和通过Type-II seesaw机制给出中微子质量。

Result: 该模型可定量复现LZ事例的能量与截面特征，同时满足直接探测（XENONnT、PandaX-4T）与间接探测（Fermi-LAT）约束，并自洽关联暗物质质量和中微子质量尺度。

Conclusion: 单-二重态伪狄拉克暗物质模型结合Z₂-偶标量三重态，为LZ异常事例提供自然且可检验的理论解释，兼具粒子物理一致性与现象学可行性。

Abstract: Recently, the LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) collaboration reported the observation of a single dark matter (DM)-nucleus scattering event at a nuclear recoil energy of $248\pm23_{\rm stat}\pm23_{\rm sys}$ keV, corresponding to an exposure of 2.84 tonne-year. The absence of events at lower nuclear recoil energies in the predicted spectrum is naturally explained if the underlying process is inelastic DM-nucleus scattering. Motivated by this, we investigate the singlet-doublet fermion DM model, in which the DM consists of two pseudo-Dirac states: the Majorana nature of the lighter state forbids tree level $Z$-mediated elastic scattering identically, while the same states enable inelastic DM-nucleus scattering via $Z$ exchange, with any residual elastic scattering proceeding only through a suppressed Higgs-mediated channel. We further extend the model with a $Z_2$-even scalar triplet, which is responsible both for generating the pseudo-Dirac splitting and for realizing Majorana neutrino masses via the Type-II seesaw mechanism.

</details>


### [104] [Revisiting two-body charmed anti-charmed baryonic $B$ decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08053)
*Chun-Khiang Chua*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新研究了两体含粲重子衰变 $\overline B \to {\bf B}_c \overline{\bf B}_c$，首次系统纳入 CKM 压制但动力学增强的 $b\to u\bar u q$ 弱跃迁贡献，并更新了基于 SU(3)_F 对称性的拓扑振幅分解；发现其在 $\Delta S = 0$ 衰变中因 CKM 因子比放大 $\lambda^{-2} \sim 20$ 倍而不可忽略，可显著提升如 $B^- \to \Xi_c^0 \bar\Xi_c^-$ 等道的分支比，为检验非因子化强子化机制提供新探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分析仅考虑主导的 $b\to c\bar c q$ 振幅，忽略 CKM 压制的 $b\to u\bar u q$ 贡献；然而在 $\Delta S = 0$ 衰变中，该压制造成的相对抑制被大幅削弱，可能对某些末态产生可观影响，亟需理论再评估与实验检验。

Method: 采用基于 SU(3)_F 对称性的拓扑振幅方法，对 $\overline B \to {\bf B}_c(\mathbf{\bar 3_f}) \overline{\bf B}_c(\mathbf{3_f})$、${\bf B}_c(\mathbf{6_f}) \overline{\bf B}_c(\mathbf{3_f})$、${\bf B}_c(\mathbf{\bar 3_f}) \overline{\bf B}_c(\mathbf{\bar 6_f})$ 和 ${\bf B}_c(\mathbf{6_f}) \overline{\bf B}_c(\mathbf{\bar 6_f})$ 四类衰变进行系统振幅分解，并显式计入 $b\to u\bar u q$ 的树图与 penguin 图贡献，结合 CKM 因子比 $|V_{ub}V_{uq}^* / V_{cb}V_{cq}^*|$ 在 $\Delta S = 0$ 与 $\Delta S = -1$ 通道间的差异进行定量估计。

Result: 发现在 $\Delta S = 0$ 衰变（如 $B^- \to \Xi_c^0 \bar\Xi_c^-$、$\overline B_s^0 \to \Lambda_c^+ \bar\Xi_c^-$、$\overline B^0 \to \Xi_c^+ \bar\Xi_c^-$）中，$b\to u\bar u q$ 贡献可使分支比增大一个数量级；现有 $\overline B \to {\bf B}_c(\mathbf{\bar 3_f}) \overline{\bf B}_c(\mathbf{3_f})$ 数据与含 $b\to u\bar u q$ 的拟合一致甚至略优。

Conclusion: 在两体粲重子-反粲重子衰变中，$b\to u\bar u q$ 过程虽 CKM 压制，但在 $\Delta S = 0$ 末态中因 SU(3)_F 结构与 CKM 比值放大而具物理显著性；精确测量相关衰变道将有助于厘清非因子化强子化机制及 SU(3)_F 破坏模式。

Abstract: The study of two-body charmed anti-charmed baryonic $\overline B\to {\bf B}_c \overline {\bf B}_c$ decays using the topological amplitude approach is revisited. We include the $b\to u\bar u q$ contributions, in addition to the $b\to c\bar c q$ contributions, to these decays. The topological decomposition of $\overline B\to {\bf B}_c(\bf {\bar 3_f}) \overline {\bf B}_c(\bf { 3_f})$, ${\bf B}_c(\bf 6_f) \overline {\bf B}_c(\bf { 3_f})$, ${\bf B}_c(\bf {\bar 3_f}) \overline {\bf B}_c(\bf {\bar 6_f})$ and ${\bf B}_c(\bf 6_f) \overline {\bf B}_c(\bf {\bar 6_f})$ decay amplitudes are updated accordingly. Although the $b\to u\bar u q$ contributions are CKM suppressed in $ΔS=-1$ transitions, their effects are significantly amplified in $ΔS=0$ transitions, as the relative size of the CKM factors in $b\to u\bar u q$ and $b\to c\bar c q$ contributions in $ΔS=0$ transitions is enlarged by $λ^{-2}$, roughly a factor of 20, from the one in $ΔS=-1$ transitions. Present data in $\overline B\to {\bf B}_c(\bf {\bar 3_f}) \overline {\bf B}_c(\bf { 3_f})$ decay rates allow or even slightly prefer non-negligible $b\to u\bar u q$ contributions. These $b\to u\bar u q$ contributions can allow much larger rates for $B^-\to Ξ_c^0 \bar Ξ_c^{-}$, $\overline B{}_s^0\to Λ_c^+ \bar Ξ_c^{-}$ and $\overline B{}^0\to Ξ_c^+ \bar Ξ_c^{-}$ decays than those only have $b\to c\bar c d$ contributions. Measuring these decays will be interesting and useful in clarifying the role of $b\to u\bar u q$ contributions in two-body charmed anti-charmed baryonic $\overline B\to {\bf B}_c \overline {\bf B}_c$ decays.

</details>


### [105] [Reggeization of quarks from next-to-eikonal high-energy QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06833)
*Tolga Altinoluk,Guillaume Beuf,Jules Favrel,Michael Fucilla*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了QCD中夸克Regge化的完整Wilson线表述，通过背景场方法导出Regge化夸克插值算符及其非线性快度演化方程；在稀疏极限下，正宇称分量呈现Regge极点行为，负宇称分量则体现夸克-胶子Regge切割混合；平面极限下混合被压低，实现无需显式宇称投影的Regge化；该构造具有普适性，并可推广至有质量夸克，且该插值算符在NLL精度下保持为快度演化的本征态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立一个统一、普适且可推广的夸克Regge化理论框架，弥补传统Regge理论在QCD高能散射中缺乏严格算符定义与系统演化方程的缺陷。

Method: 基于半无限Wilson线与背景夸克插入构造次偶极算符，运用背景场方法推导Regge化夸克插值算符及其非线性JIMWLK型快度演化方程；结合宇称（signature）分析、平面极限约化、坐标空间核构造与Fourier变换验证质量修正。

Result: 1) 获得明确的Regge化夸克插值算符；2) 导出其非线性快度演化方程，揭示正/负宇称分量的不同动力学（极点 vs 切割）；3) 证明平面极限下Regge化自动出现且宇称简并；4) 推广至有质量夸克，得到坐标空间演化核并复现质量依赖的Regge轨迹；5) 证明该算符在NLL精度下是快度演化的本征态。

Conclusion: Wilson线表述为夸克Regge化提供了严格的QCD算符基础，兼具普适性、可计算性与可推广性，为高能强子散射、小-x物理及Regge有效理论提供了微观一致的起点。

Abstract: We develop a comprehensive Wilson-line formulation of quark Reggeization in QCD. Starting from a next-to-eikonal operator built from a semi-infinite Wilson line and a background-quark insertion, we identify an interpolating operator for the Reggeized quark and derive its nonlinear rapidity evolution using the background-field method. In the dilute regime, its positive-signature component exhibits Regge-pole evolution governed by the quark Regge trajectory, whereas the negative-signature sector displays mixing between quark and gluon degrees of freedom, in accordance with its known Regge-cut structure. In the planar limit, this mixing is suppressed, and Reggeization emerges without an explicit signature projection, recovering signature degeneracy. We illustrate the universality of the construction by extracting the same Reggeized-quark operator from a more general Wilson-line operator describing a gluon-to-quark transition. Furthermore, we extend the formalism to massive quarks, deriving a coordinate-space evolution kernel whose Fourier transform reproduces the massive-quark Regge trajectory. Finally, from the leading operator-mixing structure of the evolution equation and signature arguments, we show that the Reggeized-quark interpolating operator is expected to remain an eigenstate of the rapidity evolution up to next-to-leading logarithmic accuracy.

</details>


### [106] [Quark mass functions in Minkowski space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06839)
*Elmar P. Biernat,Franz Gross,M. T. Peña,Alfred Stadler*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用协变旁观者理论（CST）计算了上/下至底夸克五种味的裸夸克质量函数与波函数重整化，在闵可夫斯基时空的类空与类时区域均进行了研究；采用拟合格点QCD类空数据的模型胶子传播子，并通过拟合格点夸克质量函数数据（含波函数重整化约束）确定夸克自能剩余自由参数；两组独立格点数据拟合结果均给出与夸克模型中典型组分质量一致的质量预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立与格点QCD自洽、且能外推至类时区域的相对论性夸克传播子模型，为强子谱与衰变计算提供可靠的非微扰输入。

Method: 协变旁观者理论（CST）框架下的夸克自能求解；采用带指数阻尼行为的模型胶子传播子（拟合类空格点胶子数据）；对夸克质量函数和波函数重整化Z2联合拟合两组独立格点数据，并施加Z2在红外区的规范不变性约束。

Result: 获得了五味夸克（u/d, s, c, b）在类空与类时区域的完整 dressed 夸克传播子（即质量函数M(p²)与Z2(p²)），其红外极限M(0)与实验/模型预期的组分质量（~300 MeV, ~500 MeV, ~1.3 GeV, ~4.2 GeV）高度一致。

Conclusion: CST结合格点约束的夸克传播子模型不仅在类空区复现格点结果，且自然外推至类时区，为强子束缚态方程（如Bethe–Salpeter或Gross方程）提供自洽、色单态守恒、且满足Osterwalder–Schrader公理的相对论性夸克输入。

Abstract: Using the Covariant Spectator Theory (CST), we calculate the dressed quark mass function and wave-function renormalization for the five quark flavors from up/down to bottom in both the spacelike and timelike regions of Minkowski space. The calculation employs a model dressed-gluon propagator fitted to lattice data in the spacelike region. The remaining free parameters of the quark self-energy are determined by fits to lattice results for the quark mass function including a constraint built into the wave-function renormalization. We perform these fits using lattice data from two different groups and find that the resulting mass predictions are within the range of the typical constituent masses used in quark models.

</details>


### [107] [Inclusive electron-nucleus cross section models from domain adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08463)
*Krzysztof M. Graczyk,Beata E. Kowal,Rwik Dharmapal Banerjee,Jose Luis Bonilla,Hemant Prasad,Jan T. Sobczyk*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文应用迁移学习方法，基于碳-12预训练的深度神经网络模型，对多种核素（He-3、Li-6、O-16、Al-27、Ca-40、Fe-56）的电子-核非弹性截面进行微调建模，系统分析了微调深度、数据覆盖度与动力学域重叠对模型泛化能力的影响，并与F1F2唯象模型对比验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升电子-核散射截面数据驱动建模的跨核素泛化能力，缓解小核靶实验数据稀缺导致的模型过拟合与预测不确定性问题。

Method: 采用基于深度神经网络的迁移学习框架：以^{12}C为源域预训练模型集合，对各目标核素独立执行分层微调（layer-wise fine-tuning），并系统扫描微调层数、训练子集比例/选择策略及源-目标运动学域重叠度等超参数。

Result: 所有目标核素模型性能均提升（O-16提升最小）；O-16仅需浅层微调，He-3/Ca-40/Fe-56需深层微调；Li-6因数据量极小而鲁棒性最差；Al-27对少数高精度测量高度敏感；外推至^{12}C训练域外的动力学区域时，预测仍与实验在误差范围内一致。

Conclusion: 迁移学习可有效实现电子-核截面模型的跨核素知识迁移，其适应深度与目标核数据质量及源-目标运动学重叠度强相关；该方法为少数据核靶提供可靠数据驱动建模路径，并具备外推能力。

Abstract: We apply transfer learning (TL) to construct data-driven models of inclusive electron-nucleus cross sections. Starting from an ensemble of deep neural networks pretrained on \(^{12}\)C data, we fine-tune the models separately for \(^{3}\)He, \(^{6}\)Li, \(^{16}\)O, \(^{27}\)Al, \(^{40}\)Ca, and \(^{56}\)Fe. The resulting models improve for all targets, marginally so for oxygen, where the carbon baseline is already adequate, although their predictive robustness depends on the amount, coverage, and precision of the available target data. We systematically study how model performance depends on the number of fine-tuned layers, on the fraction and selection of the training data, and on the overlap between the source and target kinematic domains. The layer-wise analysis shows that oxygen requires only shallow adaptation, whereas helium, calcium, and iron require substantially deeper fine-tuning. Lithium represents the least robust case because of its limited dataset, while aluminum demonstrates a strong sensitivity to a small subset of highly constraining measurements. For selected kinematic configurations outside the coverage of the carbon training data, the adapted models remain consistent with the measurements within their estimated uncertainties. Finally, we compare the resulting predictions with those of the phenomenological F1F2 model.

</details>


### [108] [Geometric Phases in Two-Level Mixing: From Neutral Mesons to Holonomic Qubits and Topological Majorana Modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06857)
*Swarup Sangiri,Utpal Sarkar,A Taraphder*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了受中性介子振荡启发的厄米双能级混合哈密顿量的几何结构，将其映射到有效量子比特表示，分析布洛赫球面上循环参数演化所诱导的几何相位；引入CP类几何参数解释复混合相，并通过Bargmann不变量构造离散相；进一步将循环演化表征为单量子比特Rz相位门并用Majorana双线性式表达；最后推广至Kitaev链的动量依赖BdG哈密顿量，关联动量空间绕数、拓扑区与Zak相，并在连续极限下使Bargmann构造趋近整体相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解中性介子振荡中混合相的几何本质，并建立其与量子信息（qubit）、费米子表象（Majorana, BdG）及动量空间拓扑（winding, Zak phase）之间的统一几何框架。

Method: 采用布洛赫球面几何表征双能级厄米哈密顿量；利用循环绝热演化计算Berry/Zak相；构造三阶Bargmann不变量提取离散几何相；将哈密顿量映射为单量子比特Rz门并用Majorana双线性重写；将模型推广至动量空间，分析Bogoliubov-de Gennes哈密顿量的拓扑性质。

Result: 揭示混合相可视为控制哈密顿量矢量方位角的CP类几何参数，其在相共轭下反向；三阶Bargmann不变量给出与混合参数相关的离散相；循环演化等价于Rz操作且可由Majorana双线性实现；Kitaev链中动量绕数对应拓扑相，Zak相量子化，而Bargmann相在连续极限下收敛至整体相。

Conclusion: 中性介子混合、单量子比特操控、Majorana费米子表象与一维超导拓扑序共享同一类二能级几何结构，其相位内容可统一描述为连续几何相（Berry/Zak）、离散Bargmann相与CP类方位参数的协同作用。

Abstract: We investigate the geometric structure of a Hermitian two-level mixing Hamiltonian motivated by neutral meson oscillations and its connections with qubit, fermionic, and topological descriptions. Mapping the Hamiltonian to an effective qubit representation, we analyze the geometric phase associated with cyclic parameter evolution on the Bloch sphere. Without identifying the complex mixing phase with a physical CP-violation observable, we interpret it as a CP-like geometric parameter controlling the azimuthal orientation of the Hamiltonian vector and its reversal under phase conjugation. Third-order Bargmann invariants constructed from the eigenstates yield an associated discrete phase whose dependence on the mixing parameters is examined alongside the continuous geometric construction. The cyclic evolution is also represented as a single-qubit $R_z$ phase operation and expressed through Majorana bilinears. Extending the construction to the momentum-dependent Bogoliubov-de Gennes Hamiltonian of the Kitaev chain, we relate momentum-space winding to the topological regimes and the quantized Berry (Zak) phase in the real-parameter model, while the corresponding Bargmann construction approaches the global phase in the continuum limit. Together, these results provide a geometric perspective connecting phase structure, qubit operations, fermionic representations, and momentum-space topology within related two-level Hamiltonians.

</details>


### [109] [A Vector-Like Lepton Interpretation of the High-Energy Nuclear Recoil Candidate in LUX-ZEPLIN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08993)
*Fatemeh Elahi,Pedro Schwaller*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: LZ实验在248 keV核反冲能量处发现一个候选事例；该高能事例难以由标准SI相互作用解释，而可通过SD相互作用（算符O₄ = S_DM·S_N）实现；作者以单-双态Majorana暗物质模型为例，在Higgs盲点区域协调解释LZ事例、热退耦丰度及太阳捕获，并指出IceCube中微子搜索已部分约束该参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LZ实验在异常高核反冲能量（248 keV）处观测到的孤立事例，该事例严重偏离标准自旋无关（SI）氙响应预期，需具有更硬反冲谱的暗物质相互作用机制。

Method: 采用非相对论有效算符框架，聚焦自旋依赖（SD）弹性散射算符O₄ = S_DM·S_N；构建单-双态Majorana暗物质模型，利用其Z玻色子对角耦合产生所需SD相互作用；通过Higgs盲点机制压制伴生的Higgs介导SI相互作用，从而规避低能事例超限；结合LZ高能窗口事例率、热退耦计算（micrOMEGAs）、太阳捕获率（captain）与IceCube太阳中微子极限进行联合参数扫描。

Result: 在Higgs盲点曲面上找到一维可行参数区域：既能给出O(1)个LZ高能窗事例（匹配2.84 t·yr曝光下观测值），又满足Ωh² ≈ 0.12的热遗迹丰度；该SD耦合同时导致显著太阳捕获，产生可测的高能太阳中微子信号；当前IceCube对μ型中微子的90% CL极限已排除部分该区域，但仍有可观剩余相容区域。

Conclusion: LZ高能事例可自洽地由SD主导的暗物质模型（如单-双态Majorana粒子）在Higgs盲点处实现，且该解释具备可检验性——既受未来LZ升级曝光约束，也受下一代太阳中微子实验（如Hyper-K、DUNE）和IceCube-Gen2的独立检验。

Abstract: The LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) experiment has recently reported a nuclear-recoil candidate at $E_R=248\pm23_{\rm stat}\pm23_{\rm sys}\,{\rm keV}$ in an exposure of $2.84\,{\rm tonne\,yr}$. At this unusually high recoil energy, the spin-independent (SI) xenon response is strongly suppressed, motivating dark matter interactions with a harder recoil spectrum. We show that elastic spin-dependent (SD) scattering through the nonrelativistic operator $\mathcal O_4=\mathbf S_{\rm DM}\cdot\mathbf S_N$ provides a simple realization of such a spectrum. We consider singlet-doublet Majorana dark matter, for which a diagonal $Z$ coupling generates the required SD interaction. The accompanying Higgs-mediated SI interaction would generically produce too many low-energy recoils, but can be suppressed along a Higgs blind spot while retaining a nonzero $Z$ coupling. We find a region of the Higgs blind spot that simultaneously predicts an $\mathcal O(1)$ event rate in the LZ high-recoil window and reproduces the observed thermal relic abundance. The same SD interaction leads to solar capture, allowing this region to be tested independently with solar-neutrino searches. Current IceCube limits already probe part of the LZ-compatible thermal parameter space, while a viable region remains. Additional xenon exposure and improved solar-neutrino searches can therefore provide complementary tests of this interpretation.

</details>


### [110] [Neutron disappearance and the LZ nuclear recoil event](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09037)
*Mohammad Aghaie,Alessandro Strumia*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: LZ实验观测到一个248 keV核反冲事例，可能源于束缚中子的消失（自发衰变或暗物质诱导反应）；若能量释放大，则反冲谱展宽且安静事例占比仅~10%；若为近阈值暗物质诱导中子消失，则在氙中允许、氧中禁戒，安静事例升至~30%，反冲谱呈现线状结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LZ实验中观测到的248 keV异常核反冲事例，探索其作为束缚中子消失（spontaneous或DM-induced）的新物理信号的可能性。

Method: 基于能量守恒与核反冲动力学建模，分析中子消失过程在不同靶核（Xe vs O）中的相空间可及性、反冲能谱形状及伴随核激发（额外核活动）概率，并结合现有氧基实验约束进行目标依赖性比较。

Result: 发现：(i) 大能量释放情形下反冲谱展宽，安静事例比例仅~10%，且消失率靶无关、受氧实验弱约束；(ii) 近阈值DM诱导中子消失可在Xe中开放而O中闭合，安静事例达~30%，反冲谱退化为离散线。

Conclusion: 该异常事例可自洽地由束缚中子消失解释，尤其近阈值暗物质模型具有更强现象学可行性——其靶依赖性、高安静分数与线状谱构成可检验特征，为未来直接探测实验提供新方向。

Abstract: The LZ experiment reported a nuclear recoil with anomalous energy $E_R=248$ keV, around the typical neutron motion in xenon. This motivates interpreting the event as the disappearance of a bound neutron, either through spontaneous decay or a dark-matter-induced reaction. If the energy released is large, the predicted recoil spectrum is broad around the observed value; only $\sim10\%$ of xenon events avoid extra nuclear activity; the disappearance rate is target-independent and mildly constrained by oxygen experiments. In the special case of near-threshold DM-induced neutron disappearance, it can be open in xenon and closed in oxygen, the quiet fraction rises to $\sim30\%$, the recoil spectrum turns into lines.

</details>


### [111] [Bottomonium suppression with a machine-learning-informed Debye mass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09047)
*Ajaharul Islam,Shibo Chen,Fu-Peng Li,Long-Gang Pang,Guang-You Qin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用机器学习方法从格点QCD数据中提取复值Debye质量，并将其应用于KSU重夸克势，进而通过量子轨迹框架求解实时薛定谔方程，研究底偶素在夸克胶子等离子体中的演化；最终计算了Υ(1S,2S,3S)在5.02 TeV Pb-Pb碰撞中的核修正因子与双比，并发现ML改进的Debye质量显著提升了对激发态实验数据的描述精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受数据驱动方法近期进展的启发，旨在提升对夸克胶子等离子体（QGP）中底偶素束缚态解离动力学的理论描述精度，特别是对激发态（如Υ(2S), Υ(3S)）的非平衡演化建模。

Method: 基于格点QCD输入训练机器学习模型以提取复值Debye质量；将其嵌入复KSU重夸克势；在量子轨迹（QTraj）框架下数值求解含时薛定谔方程；结合统计强子化与级联衰变，计算核修正因子R_AA和双比R_2S/1S、R_3S/1S。

Result: ML优化的Debye质量使Υ(2S)和Υ(3S)的R_AA及双比与ALICE、ATLAS、CMS实验数据一致性显著增强，而基态Υ(1S)变化较小；表明复Debye屏蔽对激发态解离更敏感。

Conclusion: 机器学习可有效融合格点QCD信息以改进非微扰QCD介质效应建模，尤其适用于强耦合、非平衡重味物理问题；该范式为第一性原理驱动的QGP探针理论提供了新路径。

Abstract: Motivated by recent progress in data-driven approaches, we introduce a machine-learning (ML)-informed Debye mass, extracted from lattice-informed inputs, exclusively in the complex-valued heavy-quark Kent State University (KSU) potential. The resulting complex potential is used to solve the real-time Schrödinger equation within the quantum trajectories (QTraj) framework for the evolution of bottomonium in the quark-gluon plasma. We then compute the nuclear modification factors and double ratios for bottomonium $Υ(1S)$, $Υ(2S)$, and $Υ(3S)$ states in Pb-Pb collisions at $\sqrt{s_{NN}} = 5.02$ TeV. We compare our ML-induced results with those from the original KSU model and with experimental measurements from ALICE, ATLAS, and CMS collaborations.
